024732:     const result=await api('/api/version-confirmation',{
024733:       method:'POST',

024735:     });

024739:       :null;

024742:       :null;

020395: function dockerInfoValue(value,fallback='-'){
020396:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return fallback;

020401: function dockerInfoBytes(value){
020402:   const n=Number(value);
020403:   if(!Number.isFinite(n)||n<=0)return '-';
020404:   const units=['B','KB','MB','GB','TB'];
020405:   let v=n,idx=0;
020406:   while(v>=1024&&idx<units.length-1){v/=1024;idx++;}
020407:   return `${v>=100||idx===0?v.toFixed(0):v.toFixed(1)} ${units[idx]}`;
020408: }
020409: function dockerInfoDuration(startedAt){
020410:   const start=new Date(startedAt||'');
020411:   if(Number.isNaN(start.getTime()))return '-';
020412:   let seconds=Math.max(0,Math.floor((Date.now()-start.getTime())/1000));
020413:   const days=Math.floor(seconds/86400); seconds%=86400;
020414:   const hours=Math.floor(seconds/3600); seconds%=3600;
020415:   const minutes=Math.floor(seconds/60);
020416:   const units=getDurationUnits();
020417:   if(days)return `${days} ${units.d} ${hours} ${units.h}`;
020418:   if(hours)return `${hours} ${units.h} ${minutes} ${units.m}`;
020419:   return `${minutes} ${units.m}`;
020420: }

020661: function formatBackupRemaining(expiresAt){
020662:   if(expiresAt===null||expiresAt===undefined||String(expiresAt).trim()===''){
020663:     return t('backupRetentionForever');
020664:   }
020665:   const expires=new Date(expiresAt||'');
020666:   if(Number.isNaN(expires.getTime()))return '-';
020667: 
020668:   const remainingMs=expires.getTime()-Date.now();
020669:   if(remainingMs<=0)return t('backupDeleteDue');
020670: 
020671:   const totalMinutes=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(remainingMs/60000));
020672:   const days=Math.floor(totalMinutes/1440);
020673:   const hours=Math.floor((totalMinutes%1440)/60);
020674:   const minutes=totalMinutes%60;
020675: 
020676:   const units=getDurationUnits();
020677:   if(days>0)return `${days} ${units.d}${hours>0?` ${hours} ${units.h}`:''}`;
020678:   if(hours>0)return `${hours} ${units.h}${minutes>0?` ${minutes} ${units.m}`:''}`;
020679:   return `${minutes} ${units.m}`;
020680: }

018910:   const german=String(de??'');
018911:   const english=String(en??german);
018912:   if(state.language==='de')return german||english;
018913:   if(state.language==='en')return english||german;

018918:   if((m=english.match(/^(\d+) images inspected$/i))){
018919:     return code==='fr'?`${m[1]} images analysées`:code==='pt'?`${m[1]} imagens analisadas`:`${m[1]} imágenes analizadas`;
018920:   }
018921:   if((m=english.match(/^(\d+) alternative sources found$/i))){

018923:   }
018924:   if((m=english.match(/^(\d+) alternatives$/i))){
018925:     return code==='fr'?`${m[1]} alternatives`:code==='pt'?`${m[1]} alternativas`:`${m[1]} alternativas`;
018926:   }
018927:   if((m=english.match(/^Image inspection failed:\s*(.*)$/i))){
018928:     const prefix=code==='fr'?'Échec de l’analyse de l’image : ':code==='pt'?'Falha na análise da imagem: ':'Falló el análisis de la imagen: ';
018929:     return prefix+m[1];
018930:   }
018931:   if((m=english.match(/^Image source could not be switched:\s*(.*)$/i))){
018932:     const prefix=code==='fr'?'Impossible de changer la source de l’image : ':code==='pt'?'Não foi possível alterar a fonte da imagem: ':'No se pudo cambiar la fuente de la imagen: ';
018933:     return prefix+m[1];
018934:   }

018910:   const german=String(de??'');
018911:   const english=String(en??german);

018919:     return code==='fr'?`${m[1]} images analysées`:code==='pt'?`${m[1]} imagens analisadas`:`${m[1]} imágenes analizadas`;
018920:   }

018950:

023433: async function waitForSingleAppCheck(stackKey,baselineFinishedAt,timeoutMs=15*60*1000){
023434:   const key=String(stackKey||'');
023435:   const baseline=String(baselineFinishedAt||'');
023436:   const deadline=Date.now()+Math.max(5000,Number(timeoutMs)||0);
023437: 
023438:   while(Date.now()<deadline){
023439:     const d=await loadStatus();
023440:     const scan=d&&d.scan;
023441:     const matching=matchingVerificationScan(scan,key);
023442:     const scanning=matching&&String(scan&&scan.state||'idle')==='scanning';
023443:     const finished=!!(
023444:       matching
023445:       &&!scanning
023446:       &&String(scan&&scan.finished_at||'')
023447:       &&String(scan.finished_at||'')!==baseline
023448:     );
023449:     if(finished)return true;
023450:     await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,1000));
023451:   }
023452:   return false;
023453: }
023454: async function checkSingleApp(app){
023455:   if(!app||state.checkingApps.has(app.stack_key)||scanMutationIsLocked())return;
023456: 
023457:   const stackKey=String(app.stack_key||'');
023458:   const baseline=appCheckBaseline();
023459:   state.checkingApps.add(stackKey);
023460:   state.checkBaselines.set(stackKey,baseline);
023461:   state.updateMessages.delete(stackKey);
023462:   render();
023463: 
023464:   try{
023465:     await api('/api/app-scan',{
023466:       method:'POST',
023467:       body:JSON.stringify({stack_key:stackKey})
023468:     });
023469: 
023470:     const finished=await waitForSingleAppCheck(stackKey,baseline);
023471:     if(!finished){
023472:       throw new Error(state.language==='en'?'The individual check timed out.':'Die Einzelprüfung hat das Zeitlimit überschritten.');
023473:     }
023474:   }catch(err){
023475:     if(err.message!=='auth'){
023476:       state.updateMessages.set(
023477:         stackKey,
023478:         {ok:false,kind:'check',text:t('checkDockerFailed').replace('{error}',err.message||'-')}
023479:       );
023480:     }
023481:   }finally{
023482:     state.checkingApps.delete(stackKey);
023483:     state.checkBaselines.delete(stackKey);
023484:     await loadStatus();
023485:     render();
023486:   }
023487: }

023509: function matchingVerificationScan(scan,stackKey){
023510:   const key=String(stackKey||'');
023511:   return !!(
023512:     scan
023513:     &&String(scan.scan_scope||'')==='app'
023514:     &&String(scan.scan_stack_key||'')===key
023515:   );

023517: function completeFinishedVerifications(scan){
023518:   let changed=false;
023519:   const apps=(state.data&&Array.isArray(state.data.apps))?state.data.apps:[];
023520:   const byKey=new Map(apps.map(app=>[String(app.stack_key||''),app]));
023521: 
023522:   for(const key of Array.from(state.verifyingApps)){
023523:     const pending=state.pendingPostScans.has(key)||state.pendingPostScans.has('*');
023524:     const matching=matchingVerificationScan(scan,key);
023525:     const active=matching&&String(scan&&scan.state||'idle')==='scanning';
023526:     const baseline=String(state.verificationBaselines.get(key)||'');
023527:     const finished=!!(
023528:       matching
023529:       &&String(scan&&scan.state||'idle')!=='scanning'
023530:       &&String(scan&&scan.finished_at||'')
023531:       &&String(scan.finished_at||'')!==baseline
023532:     );
023533: 
023534:     if(pending||active||!finished)continue;
023535: 
023536:     state.verifyingApps.delete(key);
023537:     state.verificationBaselines.delete(key);
023538: 
023539:     const message=state.updateMessages.get(key);
023540:     const app=byKey.get(key);
023541:     if(state.restoreVerificationApps.has(key)){
023542:       state.restoreVerificationApps.delete(key);
023543:       // No green intermediate notice while verification is running.
023544:       // Only after the targeted post-restore scan finishes, show a short success message.
023545:       showTransientRestoreSuccess(key);
023546:     }else if(message&&message.kind==='update'){
023547:       if(message.ok||appUpdateVerifiedCurrent(app)){
023548:         state.updateMessages.delete(key);
023549:       }
023550:     }
023551:     changed=true;
023552:   }
023553:   return changed;
023554: }
023555: async function waitForPostUpdateVerification(stackKey,timeoutMs=15*60*1000){
023556:   const key=String(stackKey||'');
023557:   if(!key)return false;
023558:   const deadline=Date.now()+Math.max(5000,Number(timeoutMs)||0);
023559: 
023560:   while(Date.now()<deadline){
023561:     await loadStatus();
023562:     if(!state.verifyingApps.has(key))return true;
023563:     await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,1000));
023564:   }
023565: 
023566:   // Do not leave the button permanently locked if the backend never completes
023567:   // the pending verification. Keep any diagnostic message for the user.
023568:   state.verifyingApps.delete(key);
023569:   state.verificationBaselines.delete(key);
023570:   render();
023571:   return false;
023572: }
